1e-12:\n   114\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   115\t            else:\n   116\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   117\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   118\t\n   119\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity and switch to more exploratory strategy\n   120\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   121\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   122\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   123\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   124\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   125\t        else:\n   126\t            # Multi-phase strategy scheduling for better exploration/exploitation balance\n   127\t            t_low, t_high = self.strategy_change_iter\n   128\t            if t < t_low:\n   129\t                # Early exploration\n   130\t                strategy = 'rand-to-best/1'\n   131\t            elif t < t_high:\n   132\t                # Mid optimization: adaptive pbest with archive (SHADE-style)\n   133\t                strategy = 'shade'\n   134\t            else:\n   135\t                # Late exploitation\n   136\t                strategy = 'current-to-best/1'\n   137\t\n   138\t        # Adaptive pbest pool size for current-to-pbest mutations\n   139\t        pbest_p = 0.3 * (1.0 - t) + 0.05\n   140\t\n   141\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   142\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   143\t        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n   144\t        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n   145\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   146\t        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n   147\t\n   148\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   149\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   150\t        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n   151\t        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n   152\t            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n   153\t\n   154\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n   155\t                'pbest_p': float(pbest_p), 'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\nReview the changes and make sure they are as expected. Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    75\t      use 'rand/1' or 'rand-to-best/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    76\t    - Middle iterations: moderate F/CR with 'current-to-pbest/1' (JADE/SHADE).\n    77\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    78\t      good building blocks), switch to 'current-to-best/1' for fast convergence.\n    79\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR and change strategy.\n    80\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling.\n    81\t    - Also outputs an adaptive pbest_p for the current-to-pbest/1 mutation pool.\n    82\t    \"\"\"\n    83\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.25, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.35,\n    84\t                 strategy_change_iter=(0.3, 0.7), stagnation_boost=0.15,\n    85\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    86\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    87\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    88\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    89\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    90\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    91\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    92\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    93\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    94\t\n    95\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    96\t        self.it = 0\n    97\t        self.last_gbest = None\n    98\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n    99\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   100\t\n   101\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   102\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   103\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   104\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   105\t\n   106\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   107\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   108\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   109\t\n   110\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   111\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   112\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   113\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   114\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   115\t            else:\n   116\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   117\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   118\t\n   119\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity and switch to more exploratory strategy\n   120\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   121\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   122\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   123\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   124\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   125\t        else:\n   126\t            # Multi-phase strategy scheduling for better exploration/exploitation balance\n   127\t            t_low, t_high = self.strategy_change_iter\n   128\t            if t < t_low:\n   129\t                # Early exploration\n   130\t                strategy = 'rand-to-best/1'\n   131\t            elif t < t_high:\n   132\t                # Mid optimization: adaptive pbest with archive (SHADE-style)\n   133\t                strategy = 'shade'\n   134\t            else:\n   135\t                # Late exploitation\n   136\t                strategy = 'current-to-best/1'\n   137\t\n   138\t        # Adaptive pbest pool size for current-to-pbest mutations\n   139\t        pbest_p = 0.3 * (1.0 -
